## 03_usl.sql

```sql
CREATE NAMESPACE IF NOT EXISTS lightning.metastore
```
0 rows

```sql
CREATE NAMESPACE IF NOT EXISTS lightning.metastore.rrcc
```
0 rows

```sql
COMPILE USL trialmatch DEPLOY NAMESPACE lightning.metastore.rrcc DDL
CREATE TABLE patient (
  patient_id varchar(16) PRIMARY KEY,
  mrn varchar(16),
  given_name varchar(64),
  family_name varchar(64),
  sex varchar(8),
  birth_date date,
  age_years int,
  zip varchar(5),
  lat double,
  lon double,
  miles_to_clinic double,
  ecog int,
  ecog_date date,
  demo_story varchar(200)
);
CREATE TABLE diagnosis (
  dx_id varchar(16) PRIMARY KEY,
  patient_id varchar(16) FOREIGN KEY REFERENCES patient(patient_id),
  icd10 varchar(10),
  diagnosis_text varchar(200),
  histology varchar(20),
  histology_ncit varchar(10),
  stage varchar(6),
  stage_group varchar(4),
  metastatic boolean,
  measurable_disease varchar(1),
  diagnosis_date date
);
CREATE TABLE metastasis (
  met_id varchar(16) PRIMARY KEY,
  patient_id varchar(16) FOREIGN KEY REFERENCES patient(patient_id),
  site varchar(40),
  status varchar(20),
  noted_date date
);
CREATE TABLE biomarker (
  result_key varchar(40) PRIMARY KEY,
  patient_id varchar(16) FOREIGN KEY REFERENCES patient(patient_id),
  lab_mrn varchar(16),
  gene varchar(10),
  status varchar(16),
  alteration varchar(20),
  tps double,
  result_text varchar(200),
  assay varchar(80),
  reported_date date
);
CREATE TABLE lab (
  accession varchar(16) PRIMARY KEY,
  patient_id varchar(16) FOREIGN KEY REFERENCES patient(patient_id),
  lab_mrn varchar(16),
  loinc varchar(10),
  analyte varchar(8),
  value double,
  unit varchar(16),
  uln double,
  collected_date date
);
CREATE TABLE therapy (
  med_order_id varchar(16) PRIMARY KEY,
  patient_id varchar(16) FOREIGN KEY REFERENCES patient(patient_id),
  order_text varchar(120),
  rxcui varchar(12),
  ingredient varchar(60),
  atc_code varchar(10),
  therapy_class varchar(24),
  line_of_therapy int,
  setting varchar(20),
  start_date date,
  end_date date
);
CREATE TABLE condition (
  problem_id varchar(16) PRIMARY KEY,
  patient_id varchar(16) FOREIGN KEY REFERENCES patient(patient_id),
  icd10 varchar(10),
  description varchar(200),
  category varchar(24)
);
CREATE TABLE trial (
  nct_id varchar(12) PRIMARY KEY,
  title varchar(400),
  phase varchar(40),
  overall_status varchar(24),
  sponsor varchar(200),
  last_update date,
  min_age int,
  max_age int,
  sex varchar(8),
  criteria_hash varchar(16),
  nearest_site_miles double
);
CREATE TABLE site (
  site_id varchar(24) PRIMARY KEY,
  nct_id varchar(12) FOREIGN KEY REFERENCES trial(nct_id),
  facility varchar(200),
  city varchar(80),
  state varchar(40),
  zip varchar(10),
  lat double,
  lon double,
  miles_to_clinic double
);
CREATE TABLE criterion (
  criterion_id varchar(24) PRIMARY KEY,
  nct_id varchar(12) FOREIGN KEY REFERENCES trial(nct_id),
  kind varchar(10),
  ordinal int,
  `text` varchar(2000),
  category varchar(16),
  method varchar(12),
  check_status varchar(12),
  compiled_json varchar(4000)
)
```
1 rows

```json
[

## 04_activate.sql

```sql
ACTIVATE USL TABLE lightning.metastore.rrcc.trialmatch.patient AS
SELECT p.patient_id, p.mrn, p.given_name, p.family_name, p.sex,
  CAST(p.birth_date AS DATE) AS birth_date,
  CAST(FLOOR(datediff(DATE '2026-10-26', CAST(p.birth_date AS DATE)) / 365.25) AS INT) AS age_years,
  LPAD(CAST(p.zip AS VARCHAR(5)), 5, '0') AS zip, CAST(z.lat AS DOUBLE) AS lat, CAST(z.lon AS DOUBLE) AS lon,
  (3958.8 * 2 * ASIN(SQRT(POWER(SIN(RADIANS(32.3668 - CAST(z.lat AS DOUBLE)) / 2), 2) + COS(RADIANS(CAST(z.lat AS DOUBLE))) * COS(RADIANS(32.3668)) * POWER(SIN(RADIANS(-86.3 - CAST(z.lon AS DOUBLE)) / 2), 2)))) AS miles_to_clinic,
  CAST(e.ecog_score AS INT) AS ecog, CAST(e.recorded_date AS DATE) AS ecog_date,
  p.demo_story
FROM ehr.patients p
LEFT JOIN terminology.zcta z ON LPAD(CAST(z.zip AS VARCHAR(5)), 5, '0') = LPAD(CAST(p.zip AS VARCHAR(5)), 5, '0')
LEFT JOIN ehr.ecog e ON e.patient_id = p.patient_id
```

```sql
ACTIVATE USL TABLE lightning.metastore.rrcc.trialmatch.diagnosis AS
SELECT dx.dx_id, dx.patient_id, dx.icd10, dx.diagnosis_text,
  CASE
      WHEN LOWER(dx.diagnosis_text) LIKE '%adenocarcinoma%' THEN 'adenocarcinoma'
      WHEN LOWER(dx.diagnosis_text) LIKE '%squamous%' THEN 'squamous'
      WHEN LOWER(dx.diagnosis_text) LIKE '%large cell%' THEN 'large_cell'
      ELSE 'nos' END AS histology,
  n.ncit_code AS histology_ncit,
  regexp_extract(dx.stage_text, 'Stage (I{1,3}V?[ABC]?|IV[AB]?)', 1) AS stage,
  CASE WHEN regexp_extract(dx.stage_text, 'Stage (I{1,3}V?[ABC]?|IV[AB]?)', 1) LIKE 'IV%' THEN 'IV' WHEN regexp_extract(dx.stage_text, 'Stage (I{1,3}V?[ABC]?|IV[AB]?)', 1) LIKE 'III%' THEN 'III' WHEN regexp_extract(dx.stage_text, 'Stage (I{1,3}V?[ABC]?|IV[AB]?)', 1) LIKE 'II%' THEN 'II' WHEN regexp_extract(dx.stage_text, 'Stage (I{1,3}V?[ABC]?|IV[AB]?)', 1) LIKE 'I%' THEN 'I' END AS stage_group,
  regexp_extract(dx.stage_text, 'Stage (I{1,3}V?[ABC]?|IV[AB]?)', 1) LIKE 'IV%' AS metastatic,
  CAST(dx.measurable_disease AS VARCHAR(1)) AS measurable_disease,
  CAST(dx.diagnosis_date AS DATE) AS diagnosis_date
FROM ehr.diagnoses dx
LEFT JOIN terminology.ncit_histology n ON n.histology_key = CASE
      WHEN LOWER(dx.diagnosis_text) LIKE '%adenocarcinoma%' THEN 'adenocarcinoma'
      WHEN LOWER(dx.diagnosis_text) LIKE '%squamous%' THEN 'squamous'
      WHEN LOWER(dx.diagnosis_text) LIKE '%large cell%' THEN 'large_cell'
      ELSE 'nos' END
```

```sql
ACTIVATE USL TABLE lightning.metastore.rrcc.trialmatch.biomarker AS
SELECT CONCAT(m.report_id, '-', m.gene) AS result_key, p.patient_id, m.patient_mrn AS lab_mrn,
  CASE WHEN m.gene = 'PD-L1' THEN 'PDL1' ELSE m.gene END AS gene,
  CASE WHEN m.interpretation IN ('Pathogenic', 'Positive') THEN 'detected'
       WHEN m.interpretation IN ('Not detected', 'Negative') THEN 'not_detected'
       ELSE 'indeterminate' END AS status,
  CASE
    WHEN m.result_text LIKE '%exon 19 deletion%' THEN 'exon19del'
    WHEN m.result_text LIKE '%L858R%' THEN 'L858R'
    WHEN m.result_text LIKE '%exon 20 insertion%' AND m.gene = 'EGFR' THEN 'exon20ins'
    WHEN m.result_text LIKE '%G12C%' THEN 'G12C'
    WHEN m.result_text LIKE '%G12D%' THEN 'G12D'
    WHEN m.result_text LIKE '%G12V%' THEN 'G12V'
    WHEN m.result_text LIKE '%G13C%' THEN 'G13C'
    WHEN m.result_text LIKE '%V600E%' THEN 'V600E'
    WHEN m.result_text LIKE '%exon 14%' THEN 'exon14skip'
    WHEN m.result_text LIKE '%fusion%' THEN 'fusion'
    WHEN m.interpretation = 'Pathogenic' THEN 'other'
  END AS alteration,
  CASE WHEN m.gene = 'PD-L1' THEN CAST(regexp_extract(m.result_text, 'score ([0-9.]+)%', 1) AS DOUBLE) END AS tps,
  m.result_text, m.assay, CAST(m.reported_date AS DATE) AS reported_date
FROM labsys.molecular m
LEFT JOIN ehr.patients p ON p.mrn = REPLACE(m.patient_mrn, 'MRN', '')
```

```sql
ACTIVATE USL TABLE lightning.metastore.rrcc.trialmatch.lab AS
SELECT r.accession, p.patient_id, r.patient_mrn AS lab_mrn, r.loinc,
  CASE r.loinc WHEN '751-8' THEN 'ANC' WHEN '777-3' THEN 'PLT' WHEN '718-7' THEN 'HGB' WHEN '2160-0' THEN 'CREAT'
    WHEN '2164-2' THEN 'CRCL' WHEN '1975-2' THEN 'TBILI' WHEN '1920-8' THEN 'AST' WHEN '1742-6' THEN 'ALT' END AS analyte,
  CAST(r.value AS DOUBLE) AS value, r.unit, CAST(r.ref_high AS DOUBLE) AS uln, CAST(SUBSTR(CAST(r.collected_at AS VARCHAR(30)), 1, 10) AS DATE) AS collected_date
FROM labsys.results r
LEFT JOIN ehr.patients p ON p.mrn = REPLACE(r.patient_mrn, 'MRN', '')
```

```sql

```sql
ACTIVATE USL TABLE lightning.metastore.rrcc.trialmatch.condition AS
SELECT pr.problem_id, pr.patient_id, pr.icd10, pr.description,
  CASE
    WHEN pr.icd10 IN ('J84.10', 'J84.9') THEN 'ild'
    WHEN pr.icd10 = 'J70.4' OR LOWER(pr.description) LIKE '%pneumonitis%' THEN 'pneumonitis'
    WHEN pr.icd10 LIKE 'M06%' OR pr.icd10 LIKE 'M32%' OR pr.icd10 LIKE 'K50%' OR pr.icd10 LIKE 'K51%' THEN 'autoimmune'
    WHEN pr.icd10 = 'B18.1' THEN 'hbv'
    WHEN pr.icd10 = 'B18.2' THEN 'hcv'
    WHEN pr.icd10 = 'B20' THEN 'hiv'
    WHEN pr.icd10 LIKE 'I50%' THEN 'heart_failure'
    WHEN pr.icd10 LIKE 'C%' AND pr.icd10 NOT LIKE 'C34%' THEN 'other_malignancy_active'
    WHEN pr.icd10 LIKE 'Z85%' THEN 'other_malignancy_history'
    ELSE 'other' END AS category
FROM ehr.problems pr
```

```sql
ACTIVATE USL TABLE lightning.metastore.rrcc.trialmatch.site AS
SELECT st.site_id, st.nct_id, st.facility, st.city, st.state, st.zip, CAST(st.lat AS DOUBLE) AS lat, CAST(st.lon AS DOUBLE) AS lon,
  (3958.8 * 2 * ASIN(SQRT(POWER(SIN(RADIANS(32.3668 - CAST(st.lat AS DOUBLE)) / 2), 2) + COS(RADIANS(CAST(st.lat AS DOUBLE))) * COS(RADIANS(32.3668)) * POWER(SIN(RADIANS(-86.3 - CAST(st.lon AS DOUBLE)) / 2), 2)))) AS miles_to_clinic
FROM trialfeed.sites st

st\"},{\"name\":\"biomarker\",\"columnSpecs\":[{\"name\":\"result_key\",\"dataType\":\"\\\"varchar(40)\\\"\",\"primaryKey\":{\"columns\":[]},\"dqAnnotations\":[],\"acAnnotations\":[]},{\"name\":\"patient_id\",\"dataType\":\"\\\"varchar(16)\\\"\",\"foreignKey\":{\"columns\":[],\"refTable\":[\"lightning.metastore.rrcc.trialmatch.patient\"],\"refColumns\":[\"patient_id\"]},\"dqAnnotations\":[],\"acAnnotations\":[]},{\"name\":\"lab_mrn\",\"dataType\":\"\\\"varchar(16)\\\"\",\"dqAnnotations\":[],\"acAnnotations\":[]},{\"name\":\"gene\",\"dataType\":\"\\\"varchar(10)\\\"\",\"dqAnnotations\":[],\"acAnnotations\":[]},{\"name\":\"status\",\"dataType\":\"\\\"varchar(16)\\\"\",\"dqAnnotations\":[],\"acAnnotations\":[]},{\"name\":\"alteration\",\"dataType\":\"\\\"varchar(20)\\\"\",\"dqAnnotations\":[],\"acAnnotations\":[]},{\"name\":\"tps\",\"dataType\":\"\\\"double\\\"\",\"dqAnnotations\":[],\"acAnnotations\":[]},{\"name\":\"result_text\",\"dataType\":\"\\\"varchar(200)\\\"\",\"dqAnnotations\":[],\"acAnnotations\":[]},{\"name\":\"assay\",\"dataType\":\"\\\"varchar(80)\\\"\",\"dqAnnotations\":[],\"acAnnotations\":[]},{\"name\":\"reported_date\",\"dataType\":\"\\\"date\\\"\",\"dqAnnotations\":[],\"acAnnotations\":[]}],\"unique\":[],\"foreignKeys\":[],\"checks\":[],\"ifNotExit\":false,\"namespace\":[\"lightning\",\"metastore\",\"rrcc\",\"trialmatch\"],\"dqAnnotations\":[{\"name\":\"biomarker_resolves_to_patient\",\"expression\":\"patient_id IS NOT NULL\",\"cte\":{}}],\"acAnnotations\":[],\"activateQuery\":\"SELECT CONCAT(m.report_id, '-', m.gene) AS result_key, p.patient_id, m.patient_mrn AS lab_mrn,\\n  CASE WHEN m.gene = 'PD-L1' THEN 'PDL1' ELSE m.gene END AS gene,\\n  CASE WHEN m.interpretation IN ('Pathogenic', 'Positive') THEN 'detected'\\n       WHEN m.interpretation IN ('Not detected', 'Negative') THEN 'not_detected'\\n       ELSE 'indeterminate' END AS status,\\n  CASE\\n    WHEN m.result_text LIKE '%exon 19 deletion%' THEN 'exon19del'\\n    WHEN m.result_text LIKE '%L858R%' THEN 'L858R'\\n    WHEN m.result_text LIKE '%exon 20 insertion%' AND m.gene = 'EGFR' THEN 'exon20ins'\\n    WHEN m.result_text LIKE '%G12C%' THEN 'G12C'\\n    WHEN m.result_text LIKE '%G12D%' THEN 'G12D'\\n    WHEN m.result_text LIKE '%G12V%' THEN 'G12V'\\n    WHEN m.result_text LIKE '%G13C%' THEN 'G13C'\\n    WHEN m.result_text LIKE '%V600E%' THEN 'V600E'\\n    WHEN m.result_text LIKE '%exon 14%' THEN 'exon14skip'\\n    WHEN m.result_text LIKE '%fusion%' THEN 'fusion'\\n    WHEN m.interpretation = 'Pathogenic' THEN 'other'\\n  END AS alteration,\\n  CASE WHEN m.gene = 'PD-L1' THEN CAST(regexp_extract(m.result_text, 'score ([0-9.]+)%', 1) AS DOUBLE) END AS tps,\\n  m.result_text, m.assay, CAST(m.reported_date AS DATE) AS reported_date\\nFROM labsys.molecular m\\nLEFT JOIN ehr.patients p ON p.mrn = REPLACE(m.patient_mrn, 'MRN', '')\"}]}"
 }
]

('Pathogenic', 'Positive') THEN 'detected'\\n       WHEN m.interpretation IN ('Not detected', 'Negative') THEN 'not_detected'\\n       ELSE 'indeterminate' END AS status,\\n  CASE\\n    WHEN m.result_text LIKE '%exon 19 deletion%' THEN 'exon19del'\\n    WHEN m.result_text LIKE '%L858R%' THEN 'L858R'\\n    WHEN m.result_text LIKE '%exon 20 insertion%' AND m.gene = 'EGFR' THEN 'exon20ins'\\n    WHEN m.result_text LIKE '%G12C%' THEN 'G12C'\\n    WHEN m.result_text LIKE '%G12D%' THEN 'G12D'\\n    WHEN m.result_text LIKE '%G12V%' THEN 'G12V'\\n    WHEN m.result_text LIKE '%G13C%' THEN 'G13C'\\n    WHEN m.result_text LIKE '%V600E%' THEN 'V600E'\\n    WHEN m.result_text LIKE '%exon 14%' THEN 'exon14skip'\\n    WHEN m.result_text LIKE '%fusion%' THEN 'fusion'\\n    WHEN m.interpretation = 'Pathogenic' THEN 'other'\\n  END AS alteration,\\n  CASE WHEN m.gene = 'PD-L1' THEN CAST(regexp_extract(m.result_text, 'score ([0-9.]+)%', 1) AS DOUBLE) END AS tps,\\n  m.result_text, m.assay, CAST(m.reported_date AS DATE) AS reported_date\\nFROM labsys.molecular m\\nLEFT JOIN ehr.patients p ON p.mrn = REPLACE(m.patient_mrn, 'MRN', '')\"}]}"
 }
]

st.site_id, st.nct_id, st.facility, st.city, st.state, st.zip, CAST(st.lat AS DOUBLE) AS lat, CAST(st.lon AS DOUBLE) AS lon,\\n  (3958.8 * 2 * ASIN(SQRT(POWER(SIN(RADIANS(32.3668 - CAST(st.lat AS DOUBLE)) / 2), 2) + COS(RADIANS(CAST(st.lat AS DOUBLE))) * COS(RADIANS(32.3668)) * POWER(SIN(RADIANS(-86.3 - CAST(st.lon AS DOUBLE)) / 2), 2)))) AS miles_to_clinic\\nFROM trialfeed.sites st\"},{\"name\":\"trial\",\"columnSpecs\":[{\"name\":\"nct_id\",\"dataType\":\"\\\"varchar(12)\\\"\",\"primaryKey\":{\"columns\":[]},\"dqAnnotations\":[],\"acAnnotations\":[]},{\"name\":\"title\",\"dataType\":\"\\\"varchar(400)\\\"\",\"dqAnnotations\":[],\"acAnnotations\":[]},{\"name\":\"phase\",\"dataType\":\"\\\"varchar(40)\\\"\",\"dqAnnotations\":[],\"acAnnotations\":[]},{\"name\":\"overall_status\",\"dataType\":\"\\\"varchar(24)\\\"\",\"dqAnnotations\":[],\"acAnnotations\":[]},{\"name\":\"sponsor\",\"dataType\":\"\\\"varchar(200)\\\"\",\"dqAnnotations\":[],\"acAnnotations\":[]},{\"name\":\"last_update\",\"dataType\":\"\\\"date\\\"\",\"dqAnnotations\":[],\"acAnnotations\":[]},{\"name\":\"min_age\",\"dataType\":\"\\\"integer\\\"\",\"dqAnnotations\":[],\"acAnnotations\":[]},{\"name\":\"max_age\",\"dataType\":\"\\\"integer\\\"\",\"dqAnnotations\":[],\"acAnnotations\":[]},{\"name\":\"sex\",\"dataType\":\"\\\"varchar(8)\\\"\",\"dqAnnotations\":[],\"acAnnotations\":[]},{\"name\":\"criteria_hash\",\"dataType\":\"\\\"varchar(16)\\\"\",\"dqAnnotations\":[],\"acAnnotations\":[]},{\"name\":\"nearest_site_miles\",\"dataType\":\"\\\"double\\\"\",\"dqAnnotations\":[],\"acAnnotations\":[]}],\"unique\":[],\"foreignKeys\":[],\"checks\":[],\"ifNotExit\":false,\"namespace\":[\"lightning\",\"metastore\",\"rrcc\",\"trialmatch\"],\"dqAnnotations\":[{\"name\":\"trial_is_recruiting\",\"expression\":\"overall_status = 'RECRUITING'\",\"cte\":{}}],\"acAnnotations\":[],\"activateQuery\":\"SELECT t.nct_id, t.title, t.phase, t.overall_status, t.sponsor, CAST(t.last_update AS DATE) AS last_update,\\n  CAST(t.min_age AS INT) AS min_age, CAST(t.max_age AS INT) AS max_age, t.sex, t.criteria_hash,\\n  CAST(t.nearest_site_miles AS DOUBLE) AS nearest_site_miles\\nFROM trialfeed.trials t\"}]}"
 }
]
```

## Live night through Zetaris

Run `night-20261027-202347` with `SAMPLE_MODE=false`: every patient check ran as SQL inside Zetaris against the `lightning.metastore.rrcc.trialmatch` semantic layer. Trace: [logs/night-20261027-202347.jsonl](../logs/night-20261027-202347.jsonl).

- Screener: 106 of 106 trial queries returned from Zetaris (106 trial queries, 25440 patient-trial pairs evaluated); median 4624 ms per trial query.
- Steward, data-quality rules run in Zetaris:
  - lab_row_resolves_to_patient on lab: 6 invalid rows (excluded from screening)
  - lab_has_unit_and_value on lab: 9 invalid rows (excluded from screening)
  - biomarker_resolves_to_patient on biomarker: 0 invalid rows
  - biomarker_has_assay_and_date on biomarker: 0 invalid rows
  - therapy_class_resolved on therapy: 0 invalid rows
  - trial_is_recruiting on trial: 0 invalid rows
- Verifier: 400 open matches re-read against source rows, 0 sent back for a human read
- Navigator: World Model: 19 new matches, 7 changed, 0 dropped since the last run; 240 patients with at least one trial to review; 283 patients one data item away
- Result: 10,266 pairs ruled out with evidence and 15,174 needing a person. The same night in sample mode (DuckDB over the same source files) gives 10,285 and 15,155.

| Trial | Checks in one Zetaris query | ms |
|---|---:|---:|
| NCT05967689 | 5 | 3232 |
| NCT05208762 | 5 | 1999 |
| NCT06452277 | 1 | 2352 |
| NCT07288177 | 5 | 2206 |
| NCT07659782 | 8 | 7931 |
